> We are building a highly optimized local automation tool called "memoQ LLM    
  Harness" (app.py) from scratch in this directory. The tool orchestrates a     
  hybrid translation/editing pipeline using the memoQ bilingual XML format      
  (*.mqxliff) and ANY OpenAI-compatible endpoint.                               
                                                                                
  Host Hardware Context: Macbook Pro M2 Max (96GB RAM, 2TB SSD).                
  VM Environment: Windows 11 ARM VM (via Parallels).                            
  Ollama Setup: Ollama is installed both in VM and macOS host. The VM Ollama    
  instance is symlinked/tunneled via SSH to the macOS host to fully leverage    
  the M2 Max 96GB RAM and shares the same local models directory. It exposes a  
  standard local endpoint at http://localhost:11434/v1 inside the VM.           
                                                                                
  Please automatically build the entire codebase in this directory with the     
  following specifications:                                                     
                                                                                
  1. MQXLIFF (XML) Parser & File Management Layer:                              
     - Use standard xml.etree.ElementTree or lxml to handle memoQ standard      
  XLIFF namespaces cleanly (xmlns="urn:oasis:names:tc:xliff:document:1.2" and   
  memoq extensions).                                                            
     - Filter segments dynamically: extract ONLY segments where                 
  confirmed!="true" and locked!="true". Skip all confirmed/locked rows.         
     - Clean/isolate inline memoQ tags (<bpt>, <ept>, <ph>) via regex so they   
  don't break prompt markdown formatting, but track their exact positions to    
  restore them perfectly during re-export.                                      
     - Create a dedicated export folder logic where the modified mqxliff file   
  is saved automatically upon clicking export.                                  
  2. State Management & Automated Feedback Loop (state.json):                   
     - Track active project data: Hard Rules (Glossary CSV), Soft Rules         
  (stylistic "vibe"), and Segment History.                                      
     - Slice unconfirmed segments into batches of 100.                          
     - AUTOMATIC DIFF CALCULATION: When the user transitions from Batch N to    
  Batch N+1, the script must automatically evaluate the user's manual inputs in 
  the grid. If modified by a human, automatically generate a structured         
  feedback block: <[segment_id]: "[Human_Final_Text]">.                         
     - Inject this diff block, along with the current rules and vibe, into the  
  system instructions for the next API batch pull to enable dynamic In-Context  
  Learning (ICL).                                                               
  3. Flexible API & Local Model Client Layer:                                   
     - Implement the client using the standard openai Python SDK.               
     - Provide pre-configured environment presets in the Streamlit Sidebar:     
       - "OpenAI API" (Default: https://openai.com, fields for API Key, default 
  model like gpt-4o or your Terra deployment).                                  
       - "Alternative Paid APIs" (OpenRouter / DeepSeek base URLs).             
       - "Local Engine (Ollama)" (Pre-sets Base URL to                          
  http://localhost:11434/v1, leaves API Key optional/blank, allows typing any   
  local model name).                                                            
  4. Advanced Split-Screen Streamlit UI Layout:                                 
     - Use a wide layout (st.set_page_config(layout="wide")).                   
     - Create a Two-Column Layout for the main workspace:                       
       - LEFT PANEL (Scrollable/Fixed):                                         
         - Section 1: Hard Rules List (loaded from config/glossary).            
         - Section 2: Current stylistic "Vibe" / Soft Rules.                    
         - Section 3: AI Analysis of previous human corrections/diffs (if       
  available from previous batch states).                                        
       - RIGHT PANEL (Main Workspace Grid):                                     
         - Output the processed file segments as an interactive, row-by-row     
  layout matching the active batch of 100.                                      
         - For each row, display: Segment ID, Source Text, and Current Target   
  (memoQ AGT MT).                                                               
         - Immediately to the right of each segment row, provide an editable    
  User Input Text Field (initialized with the LLM's new suggestion) and a small 
  User Comment input field (to feed manual context/instructions back to the     
  model for the next prompt iteration).                                         
     - Control buttons placed cleanly at the bottom/sidebar: "Process Current   
  Batch", "Apply Changes & Calculate Diffs", "Export Valid MQXLIFF".            
                                                                                
  Please generate the complete codebase, automatically install required         
  packages (streamlit, openai, lxml) using pip, and create a robust, modular,   
  production-ready 'app.py' file without placeholders.

    synthetic mqxliff file

  One thing I want to flag, since it affects the module I'm writing next: your
  spec says to filter on confirmed!="true" and locked!="true", but memoQ's
  actual mqxliff output varies by version — some emit mq:status="Confirmed" on
  the trans-unit, others use mq:confirmed/mq:locked attributes, and the <target 
  state="..."> attribute is a third signal. I'm going to implement your stated
  rule as the primary check and add the others as fallbacks, with the filter
  visible/overridable in the UI, rather than betting on one layout. If you have
  a real .mqxliff handy, dropping it in this directory would let me verify
  against the genuine article instead of my synthetic fixture.

● Auto mode lets Claude handle permission prompts automatically — Claude
  checks each tool call for risky actions and prompt injection before 
  executing. Actions Claude identifies as safe are executed, while 
  actions Claude identifies as risky are blocked and Claude may try a 
  different approach. Ideal for long-running tasks. Sessions are 
  slightly more expensive. Claude can make mistakes that allow harmful 
  commands to run, it's recommended to only use in isolated 
  environments. Shift+Tab to change mode.

      165 +def isolate_pair(source_xml: str, target_xml: str) -> Tuple[Ta
          +ggedText, TaggedText, TaggedText]:                            
      166 +    """Isolate a source/target pair under one shared placehold
          +er numbering.                                                 
      167 +                                                              
      168 +    Isolating the two sides independently would be a correctne
          +ss trap: ``{1}``                                              
      169 +    in the source and ``{1}`` in the target would be numbered 
          +per-call and                                                  
      170 +    could stand for different tags, so a model asked to "keep 
          +the tags" would                                               
      171 +    silently swap them.  Here the source defines the numbering
          +, a target tag                                                
      172 +    reuses its source twin's number when the XML is identical,
          + and any                                                      
      173 +    target-only tag is given a fresh number above the source r
          +ange.                                                         
      174 +                                                              
      175 +    Returns ``(source, target, lookup)`` where ``lookup`` carr
          +ies the union of                                              
      176 +    both tag maps and is what :func:`restore` should resolve a
          +gainst.                                                       
      177 +    """                                                       
      178 +    source_pieces = _tokenize(source_xml)                     
      179 +    target_pieces = _tokenize(target_xml)                     
      180 +    scheme_name, template, _ = _pick_scheme(                  
      181 +        [p for is_tag, p in source_pieces + target_pieces if n
          +ot is_tag]                                                    
      182 +    )                                                         
      183 +                                                              
      184 +    source_tags: Dict[int, str] = {}                          
      185 +    source_out: List[str] = []                                
      186 +    for is_tag, payload in source_pieces:                     
      187 +        if is_tag:                                            
      188 +            number = len(source_tags) + 1                     
      189 +            source_tags[number] = payload                     
      190 +            source_out.append(template % number)              
      191 +        else:                                                 
      192 +            source_out.append(unescape_text(payload))         
      193 +                                                              
      194 +    union_tags: Dict[int, str] = dict(source_tags)            
      195 +    consumed: set = set()                                     
      196 +    target_tags: Dict[int, str] = {}                          
      197 +    target_out: List[str] = []                                
      198 +    for is_tag, payload in target_pieces:                     
      199 +        if not is_tag:                                        
      200 +            target_out.append(unescape_text(payload))         
      201 +            continue                                          
      202 +        number = next(                                        
      203 +            (n for n, xml in source_tags.items() if xml == pay
          +load and n not in consumed),                                  
      204 +            None,                                             
      205 +        )                                                     
      206 +        if number is None:                                    
      207 +            number = max(union_tags) + 1 if union_tags else 1 
      208 +        consumed.add(number)                                  
      209 +        union_tags[number] = payload                          
      210 +        target_tags[number] = payload                         
      211 +        target_out.append(template % number)                  
      212 +                                                              
      213 +    return (                                                  
      214 +        TaggedText("".join(source_out), source_tags, scheme_na
          +me),                                                          
      215 +        TaggedText("".join(target_out), target_tags, scheme_na
          +me),                                                          
      216 +        TaggedText("", union_tags, scheme_name),              
      217 +    )

      220 +def _tokenize(inner_xml: str) -> List[Tuple[bool, str]]:      
      221 +    """Split serialised segment content into ``(is_tag, payloa
          +d)`` runs."""                                                 
      222 +    pieces: List[Tuple[bool, str]] = []                       
      223 +    cursor = 0                                                
      224 +    for match in _TAG_RE.finditer(inner_xml):                 
      225 +        if match.start() > cursor:                            
      226 +            pieces.append((False, inner_xml[cursor : match.sta
          +rt()]))                                                       
      227 +        pieces.append((True, match.group(0)))                 
      228 +        cursor = match.end()                                  
      229 +    if cursor < len(inner_xml):                               
      230 +        pieces.append((False, inner_xml[cursor:]))            
      231 +    return pieces

      4 authority: the base task description, the soft "vibe", the hard g
      5 and finally the human corrections harvested from previous batches
        .  The
        r layer wins,
      7 so a correction the operator made by hand in batch 3 overrides a 
      8 instruction they wrote in batch 1 without them having to restate 
      9 """

● Root cause found: Streamlit forbids writing to st.session_state[key] after
  that widget was instantiated in the same run — my nav code did exactly that,
  and the raised exception aborted the rest of the grid. Fixing with a
  batch-scoped widget key instead:

      44      model_suggestions: Tuple[str, ...] = ()

      46  
      47 +def ollama_base_url() -> str:                                  
      48 +    """Where this machine's Ollama actually lives.             
      49 +                                                               
      50 +    ``OLLAMA_HOST`` is the variable Ollama's own CLI honours, a
         +nd it is what a                                                
      51 +    tunnelled setup sets - e.g. a Windows VM pointed at the mac
         +OS host over                                                   
      52 +    Parallels holds ``10.211.55.2:11434`` there. Defaulting bli
         +ndly to                                                        
      53 +    localhost would leave exactly that setup unable to connect.
      54 +    """                                                        
      55 +    host = (os.environ.get("OLLAMA_HOST") or "").strip()       
      56 +    if not host:                                               
      57 +        return "http://localhost:11434/v1"                     
      58 +    if not host.startswith(("http://", "https://")):           
      59 +        host = "http://" + host                                
      60 +    return host.rstrip("/") + "/v1"                            
      61 +                                                               
      62 +

● Done. The harness is built, tested, and verified end to end against your live
  Ollama.

  What's there

  app.py                  Streamlit UI (presentation only)
  memoq_harness/
    xmlcompat.py          XML shim: lxml preferred, ElementTree fallback
    tags.py               inline tag isolation/restoration
    mqxliff.py            parsing, filtering, re-export
    state.py              state.json, batching, feedback loop
    prompts.py            prompt assembly
    llm.py                OpenAI-compatible client + presets
  config/glossary.csv     sample hard rules, auto-loaded
  tests/                  90 tests

  Run it: streamlit run app.py. All 90 tests pass, pyflakes clean. Installed
  streamlit 1.64.0, openai 3.16.2, lxml 6.1.3, pandas 3.0.6.

  The one thing you'll want to know

  Your Ollama isn't on localhost. OLLAMA_HOST is 10.211.55.2:11434 — your Mac
  host through Parallels — and nothing listens on the VM's own localhost:11434.
  The spec said to hardcode http://localhost:11434/v1, which would have failed
  on your actual machine. The preset now reads OLLAMA_HOST and resolves to
  http://10.211.55.2:11434/v1 automatically, falling back to localhost when
  unset.

  I also corrected the OpenAI base URL to https://api.openai.com/v1 —
  https://openai.com serves HTML, not chat completions.

  Verified against the real model

  Full stack with qwen3:4b-instruct: load → filter (7 of 12 rows) → live
  translation (7/7 returned) → human correction → diff → export → re-parse. The
  confirmed row came through untouched; the human edit landed in the file.

  The feedback loop was checked behaviourally, not just structurally: given a
  correction teaching the short infinitive form, the model returned Bericht als 
  PDF exportieren instead of the Exportieren Sie ... phrasing it had been shown
  as rejected.

  Judgement calls worth flagging

  - Segment filtering consults four signals (mq:confirmed, mq:locked, mq:status,
    <target state>) rather than only the confirmed!="true" / locked!="true" you
    specified, because memoQ varies by version. Each is toggleable in the
    sidebar, and skip counts are always shown.
  - Source and target share one tag numbering. Numbering each side independently
    would let {1} mean different tags on each side, and a model told to "keep
    the tags" would silently swap them.
  - Feedback blocks default to including source + rejected text alongside your
    exact <[id]: "[text]"> format — a before/after pair teaches the pattern far
    better than the final string alone. Toggleable.
  - Batch (100) and display page (25) are separate. Streamlit re-runs the script
    on every interaction; 200 live text areas at once is sluggish. Processing
    and diffing still happen per whole batch.
  - API keys never touch state.json — there's a test asserting it.

  Not covered

  The paid-API presets (OpenAI, OpenRouter, DeepSeek) have had no live call made
  against them — only the shared client code that Ollama exercises. Retry and
  rate-limit branches are unit-tested, not hit with real 429s.

  One caution: my own PowerShell cleanup pass double-encoded some Unicode
  escapes mid-build (Windows PowerShell 5.1 reads files as ANSI by default). I
  caught it, repaired it, and made those lines pure ASCII — but if you ever
  bulk-rewrite these files with Get-Content/Set-Content, pass -Encoding utf8
  explicitly. There's a note in the README.

  ⎿  OK   http://localhost:11434/v1/models (5 models)
     OK   http://10.211.55.2:11434/v1/models (5 models)
     OLLAMA_HOST(user) = 10.211.55.2:11434
     app will use: http://10.211.55.2:11434/v1

● Both routes are live now, so you're fine either way.

  One detail: OLLAMA_HOST is still 10.211.55.2:11434, so the app will resolve to
  the direct host address, not your new tunnel on localhost. Both serve the
  same 5 models, so it'll just work — but it means the tunnel isn't actually in
  the path. If you want traffic going through it (e.g. so the app keeps working
  when the direct Parallels route is unavailable), either clear OLLAMA_HOST to
  fall back to localhost, or just type http://localhost:11434/v1 into the
  sidebar's Base URL field — it overrides the preset.

  A couple of things for the first real run:

  - Hit Test in the sidebar before processing anything — it confirms the
    endpoint in one round trip instead of finding out mid-batch.
  - For a real file, drop Segments per request to 5–10. It defaults to 20, which
    is fine for qwen2.5:72b but a truncated JSON response costs you the whole
    chunk.
  - If tag warnings show up in red on a row, the export is still safe — missing
    tags get reattached and the file is re-parsed before it reports success —
    but those rows are worth eyeballing.

  Good luck with it.

  Правила редактуры                                                             
  Данные не трогаем: Segment ID и Source не менять; TR confirmed и locked не    
  править, а помечать "Оставляем (TR confirmed)"; даже неизмененный сегмент

  Страховка вывода: если теги или Markdown могут исказить строку, проблемные    
  сегменты дублировать plain text вне таблицы.                                  
                                                                                
  Формат: таблица строго из пяти столбцов — ID, Source, Current Target, Edited  
  Target, Comment; использовать только короткий числовой ID. Target в рабочих   
  таблицах приводится без тегов и [mq:ch val="nbsp"].

  ### 2. Fix memoQ CSV Parser (`load_glossary_csv`)                             
  The memoQ glossary export format does not use 'source' or 'target' header     
  tags. It has the following raw byte layout:                                   
  "English,English,English,English,English,Russian,Russian" followed by 1000+   
  data rows.                                                                    
                                                                                
  Update the CSV loader in `memoq_harness/state.py` to be position-based and    
  headless:                                                                     
  - Skip row 0 entirely (the headers).                                          
  - For all following lines, extract Column 0 (index 0) as the Source English   
  Term and Column 5 (index 5) as the Target Russian Term.                       
  - Clean whitespaces, handle missing values, and support varying line breaks   
  safely to prevent the parser from dropping rows.

  `memoq_harness/state.py` and verify with internal tests.

       64  @dataclass
       65 +class TermbaseLoadReport:                                     
       66 +    """What a termbase import actually did - surfaced in the U
          +I."""                                                         
       67 +                                                              
       68 +    loaded: int = 0                                           
       69 +    header_rows: int = 0                                      
       70 +    data_rows: int = 0                                        
       71 +    blank_rows: int = 0                                       
       72 +    incomplete_rows: int = 0                                  
       73 +    duplicate_rows: int = 0                                   
       74 +    encoding: str = ""                                        
       75 +    delimiter: str = ","                                      
       76 +                                                              
       77 +    def summary(self) -> str:                                 
       78 +        bits = ["%d terms loaded" % self.loaded]              
       79 +        if self.incomplete_rows:                              
       80 +            bits.append("%d rows missing a source or target" %
          + self.incomplete_rows)                                        
       81 +        if self.duplicate_rows:                               
       82 +            bits.append("%d duplicate sources" % self.duplicat
          +e_rows)                                                       
       83 +        if self.blank_rows:                                   
       84 +            bits.append("%d blank rows" % self.blank_rows)    
       85 +        detail = "%s, delimiter %r" % (self.encoding, self.del
          +imiter)                                                       
       86 +        return "%s (%s)" % ("; ".join(bits), detail)          
       87 +                                                              
       88 +                                                              
       89 +def _decode_csv(data: bytes | str) -> tuple[str, str]:        
       90 +    """Decode CSV bytes, sniffing the encodings memoQ actually
          + emits."""                                                    
       91 +    if isinstance(data, str):                                 
       92 +        return data, "str"                                    
       93 +    if data.startswith((b"\xff\xfe", b"\xfe\xff")):           
       94 +        return data.decode("utf-16"), "utf-16"                
       95 +    for encoding in ("utf-8-sig", "utf-8", "cp1251", "latin-1"
          +):                                                            
       96 +        try:                                                  
       97 +            return data.decode(encoding), encoding            
       98 +        except UnicodeDecodeError:                            
       99 +            continue                                          
      100 +    return data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), "utf-8 (los
          +sy)"                                                          
      101 +                                                              
      102 +                                                              
      103 +def _sniff_delimiter(text: str) -> str:                       
      104 +    """Pick the delimiter from the header row; memoQ varies by
          + host locale."""                                              
      105 +    first = text.split("\n", 1)[0]                            
      106 +    counts = {d: first.count(d) for d in (",", ";", "\t")}    
      107 +    best = max(counts, key=lambda d: counts[d])               
      108 +    return best if counts[best] else ","                      
      109 +                                                              
      110 +                                                              
      111 +def _squash(value: str) -> str:                               
      112 +    """Collapse internal whitespace so a term stays on one pro
          +mpt line."""                                                  
      113 +    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", value).strip()                 
      114 +                                                              
      115 +                                                              
      116 +@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=4096)                            
      117 +def _term_pattern(term: str) -> re.Pattern:                   
      118 +    """Whole-word matcher for a termbase source term.         
      119 +                                                              
      120 +    Word boundaries are only applied where the term's own edge
          + is alphanumeric;                                             
      121 +    a term like ``C++`` or ``.mqh`` would never match otherwis
          +e.                                                            
      122 +    """                                                       
      123 +    left = r"\b" if term[:1].isalnum() else ""                
      124 +    right = r"\b" if term[-1:].isalnum() else ""              
      125 +    return re.compile(left + re.escape(term) + right, re.IGNOR
          +ECASE)                                                        
      126 +                                                              
      127 +                                                              
      128 +@dataclass                                                    
      129  class Correction:
      130      """A single human edit, distilled for in-context learning.
           """

● Now documenting the new rules/termbase split, replacing the old glossary

● Update(README.md)
  ⎿  Added 45 lines
      110    duplicates a placeholder, the row is flagged in red in the g
           rid and the export
      111    still produces a file memoQ can open (missing tags are reatt
           ached at the end).
      112  
      113 +### Hard Rules vs. Termbase                                   
      114 +                                                              
      115 +These are two different things and the distinction matters:   
      116 +                                                              
      117 +**Hard Rules** is the editorial rulebook — free prose, edited 
          +in the left panel,                                            
      118 +injected into the system prompt **verbatim and in full**. It i
          +s never                                                       
      119 +reformatted, filtered or summarised. It ships with a default i
          +n                                                             
      120 +`memoq_harness/defaults.py`; **Restore** resets to it.        
      121 +                                                              
      122 +**Termbase** is the memoQ glossary CSV — a table of term pairs
          +, filtered per                                                
      123 +batch so only the terms that actually occur are spent on token
          +s. A 1000-entry                                               
      124 +termbase would otherwise cost more than the segments themselve
          +s and bury the                                                
      125 +handful of terms that apply.                                  
      126 +                                                              
      127 +Prompt authority runs: vibe → hard rules → termbase → human co
          +rrections, with                                               
      128 +each later layer told explicitly that it wins.                
      129 +                                                              
      130 +### The memoQ termbase CSV                                    
      131 +                                                              
      132 +memoQ's export is **headless and positional**. Row 0 reads    
      133 +`English,English,English,English,English,Russian,Russian` — fi
          +ve source synonym                                             
      134 +columns, then two target ones — so there is nothing to match c
          +olumn names                                                   
      135 +against. The loader takes **column 0** as the source term and 
          +**column 5** as                                               
      136 +the target, skipping row 0 entirely.                          
      137 +                                                              
      138 +Robustness is deliberate, because a 1000-row termbase that sil
          +ently drops rows                                              
      139 +is worse than useless:                                        
      140 +                                                              
      141 +* encoding is sniffed — UTF-8 with or without BOM, UTF-16 LE/B
          +E, cp1251;                                                    
      142 +* the delimiter is sniffed from the header row (`,` `;` or tab
          +);                                                            
      143 +* parsing goes through Python's `csv` module, so quoted fields
          + containing                                                   
      144 +  commas *or embedded line breaks* stay intact — splitting on 
          +newlines by hand                                              
      145 +  is the usual way these files get corrupted;                 
      146 +* CR, LF and CRLF are all accepted; whitespace is trimmed; dup
          +licate sources                                                
      147 +  collapse.                                                   
      148 +                                                              
      149 +Every import returns a report (`34 terms loaded; 2 rows missin
          +g a source or                                                 
      150 +target; utf-8-sig, delimiter ','`) shown in the UI, so anythin
          +g skipped is                                                  
      151 +visible rather than silently lost. Column positions are overri
          +dable via                                                     
      152 +`load_termbase_csv(source_col=..., target_col=...)`.          
      153 +                                                              
      154 +Term matching is whole-word and case-insensitive — a plain sub
          +string test would                                             
      155 +fire `side` on "con**side**r" — while still matching multi-wor
          +d terms and terms                                             
      156 +with non-alphanumeric edges like `C++`.                       
      157 +                                                              
      158  ### The feedback loop
      159  
      160  When you leave a batch — via **Next** or **Apply Changes & Cal
           culate Diffs** —

      233  ### Unicode note for contributors
      234  
      235 -Keep source files ASCII and write non-ASCII characters as `\uX
          -XXX` escapes.                                                 
      236 -Windows PowerShell 5.1's `Get-Content`/`Set-Content` default t
          -o the system ANSI

          -script ever                                                   
      238 -rewrites these files.

          +berately non-ASCII

      237 +`Get-Content`/`Set-Content`** — those default to the system AN
          +SI codepage and                                               
      238 +will double-encode every Cyrillic character. Edit them with a

● Three of those are genuine conflicts, and one is a real problem with my dedup:
  Connect to an Account (a UI heading → "Подключение к счету") and connect to 
  an account (a verb phrase → "подключиться к счету") are different terms 
  distinguished only by case, and my case-insensitive dedup was silently
  dropping one. Fixing that:

● The live run surfaced a real quality problem worth fixing. Look at what your
  actual termbase matched for two ordinary MQL5 sentences:

  To    -> Кому        File -> Файл       Type -> Тип
  Magic -> Magic       Filling -> Заполнение

  Those are UI-label entries firing on ordinary prose words — and instructing
  the model to translate "to" as "Кому" would actively damage output. Two
  principled fixes:

● Update(memoq_harness\state.py)
  ⎿  Added 11 lines, removed 2 lines
      113      return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", value).strip()

      116 -@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=4096)                            
      116 +@functools.lru_cache(maxsize=8192)                            
      117  def _term_pattern(term: str) -> re.Pattern:
      118      """Whole-word matcher for a termbase source term.
      119  
      120      Word boundaries are only applied where the term's own edge
            is alphanumeric;
      121      a term like ``C++`` or ``.mqh`` would never match otherwis
           e.
      122 +                                                              
      123 +    Capitalisation is treated as meaningful. A termbase entry 
          +carrying an                                                   
      124 +    uppercase letter is a proper noun or a UI label, so it is 
          +matched                                                       
      125 +    case-sensitively; otherwise real exports wreck ordinary pr
          +ose - ``To`` ->                                               
      126 +    "Кому" and ``File`` -> "Файл" are the MetaTrader UI's labe
          +ls, and letting                                               
      127 +    them fire on the words "to" and "file" would instruct the 
          +model to                                                      
      128 +    mistranslate them. All-lowercase entries stay case-insensi
          +tive so they                                                  
      129 +    still match at the start of a sentence.                   
      130      """
      131      left = r"\b" if term[:1].isalnum() else ""
      132      right = r"\b" if term[-1:].isalnum() else ""
      125 -    return re.compile(left + re.escape(term) + right, re.IGNOR
          -ECASE)                                                        
      133 +    flags = 0 if any(c.isupper() for c in term) else re.IGNORE
          +CASE                                                          
      134 +    return re.compile(left + re.escape(term) + right, flags)  
      135  
      136  
      137  @dataclass

      592 +def _drop_subsumed(hits: Sequence[TermEntry]) -> List[TermEntr
          +y]:                                                           
      593 +    """Keep the most specific term where matches nest inside o
          +ne another.                                                   
      594 +                                                              
      595 +    "order filling type" and "filling type" can both be in a t
          +ermbase with                                                  
      596 +    different translations ("тип исполнения ордера" vs "тип за
          +ливки"). When

          +hor meant, and

          +ame for "Magic"

      608 +            for other in hits                                 
      609 +        ):                                                    
      610 +            continue                                          
      611 +        kept.append(entry)                                    
      612 +    return kept

● Refactor complete. All 134 tests pass, lint clean, verified against your real
  termbase and the live model.

  1. Left panel — Hard Rules and Vibe are now editable text blocks

  Three distinct sections, no longer conflating the glossary with hard rules:

  - Hard Rules — your editorial rulebook as free prose, injected into the prompt
    verbatim and in full (5,085 chars, ~1,271 tokens). Never reformatted or
    filtered.
  - Vibe — the article's stylistic direction (1,176 chars).
  - Termbase — the memoQ CSV, browsable and filterable, kept separate.

  Both text blocks ship with your defaults in memoq_harness/defaults.py, are
  editable in place, persist to state.json, and have a Restore button. Clearing
  a box stays cleared — an empty block is treated as a decision, not a missing
  value.

  2. CSV parser — position-based and headless

  Skips row 0, reads column 0 → source and column 5 → target. Against your real
  file:

  1250 terms loaded; 2 duplicate sources (utf-8-sig, delimiter ',')
  1252 data rows → 0 incomplete, 0 blank, 0 empty targets

  Encoding sniffing (UTF-8 ±BOM, UTF-16, cp1251), delimiter sniffing, and
  csv-module parsing so quoted commas and embedded line breaks don't drop rows.
  Every import returns a report shown in the UI. Your export auto-loads at
  startup in preference to the sample.

  Three things your real data exposed

  Case-insensitive dedup was losing entries. Connect to an Account →
  «Подключение к счету» (UI heading) and connect to an account → «подключиться к
  счету» (verb phrase) differ only by case. Dedup is now exact-case, so both
  survive — that's why the count is 1250, not 1248.

  UI labels were poisoning the prompt. Your termbase has To → «Кому», File →
  «Файл», Type → «Тип`. Case-insensitive matching fired these on ordinary prose,
  instructing the model to mistranslate "to" and "file". Capitalised entries
  now match case-sensitively; lowercase ones stay insensitive. Two MQL5
  sentences went from 10 noisy matches to 5 clean ones (~28 tokens vs ~10,500
  for the whole termbase).

  Your termbase and vibe contradict each other. The termbase maps filling type →
  «тип заливки» (the paint sense); your vibe says order filling type — тип 
  исполнения ордера. Since I'd ranked termbase above vibe, the false friend
  would have won. Your vibe calls the termbase a «терминологическая опора», so I
  framed it as general support that article-specific rules override, and told
  the model to translate by meaning when an entry is plainly the wrong sense. If
  you'd rather the termbase be absolute, that's a one-line change — say the
  word.

  One process note: I briefly broke the build by placing a module-level helper
  inside the class body, which silently orphaned ProjectState.totals and failed
  all 30 app tests at once. Caught and fixed; the suite is green.

> We are expanding the functionality of our local "memoQ LLM Harness" (app.py   
  and memoq_harness/ package). The core backend works, but we need to optimize  
  tag orchestration, punctuation processing, endpoint presets, and layout based 
  on production testing.                                                        
                                                                                
  ### 1. Direct Anthropic Claude API Preset (NEW FEATURE)                       
  - Add a native "Anthropic API" preset to the Streamlit Sidebar environment    
  dropdown (alongside OpenAI, OpenRouter, DeepSeek, and Ollama).                
  - When selected, the backend (`memoq_harness/llm.py`) must switch from the    
  `openai` SDK to the official `anthropic` Python SDK (install it via pip if    
  necessary).                                                                   
  - Provide UI fields for: "Anthropic API Key" (masked password input) and      
  "Model Name" (defaulting to `claude-3-5-sonnet-latest`).                      
  - Ensure the prompt formatting, streaming (if any), and error-handling paths  
  adapt seamlessly to Anthropic's message structure.                            
                                                                                
  ### 2. Tag Orchestration & Default Re-Targeting (CRITICAL CORRECTION)         
  - Current Behavior: The harness uses the Source segment XML to map and number 
  inline tags ({1}, {2}).                                                       
  - Problem: memoQ's pre-translation/MT layer ("Current Target") frequently     
  localizes the internal attributes of these tags (e.g., swapping English links 
  `/en/articles/` for Russian links `/ru/articles/`). Re-applying source tags   
  corrupts these localized paths.                                               
  - Solution: Refactor `memoq_harness/tags.py` and `mqxliff.py`. By default,    
  the tag lookup and initial placeholder injection MUST be derived from the     
  `Current Target` (memoQ MT) XML rather than the Source XML, preserving all    
  localized internal attributes.                                                
  - Full Tag Inspection: Modify the Streamlit grid layout. Add a small toggle   
  button or a hover tooltip to "Show Full Raw XML Tags" for each segment row,   
  so the operator can inspect exactly what code is hidden inside `{1}` or `{2}` 
  without opening memoQ.                                                        
                                                                                
  ### 3. Deterministic Punctuation & Quotes Normalization (Backend Rule)        
  - Our Hard Rules strictly state: quotes must only be straight computer quotes 
  (" "), and never typographic guillemets (« ») or curly quotes.                
  - To prevent the LLM from occasionally ignoring this rule in long strings,    
  update the deterministic text cleaning layer in `memoq_harness/state.py`      
  (where em-dashes and 'ё' letters are currently normalized).                   
  - Add a strict regex pass that automatically forces all typographic quotes    
  (`«`, `»`, `“`, `”`, `„`) inside the generated LLM Suggestion or Edited       
  Target to flatten into standard straight computer quotes (``"``) before       
  rendering the text box and during final export.                               
                                                                                
  ### 4. Pre-Editing Visual Diff Highlights (UI)                                
  - Before the operator starts manual post-editing in the "Your text" column,   
  implement an automatic inline visual diff layer.                              
  - Compare the "Current target (memoQ MT)" with the newly generated "LLM       
  Suggestion" segment-by-segment.                                               
  - Display a small formatted markdown or HTML diff block directly below or     
  inside the workspace row (using standard text deletion/addition highlights or 
  color codes).                                                                 
                                                                                
  ### 5. Collapsible Left Panel / Flexible Split-Screen Layout                  
  - Implement a collapsible configuration toggle or layout columns modifier for 
  the left work area.                                                           
  - Allow the operator to minimize or completely hide the Rules/Vibe section    
  via a single UI button ("Collapse Rules Panel") so that the Translation Grid  
  immediately expands to take up 100% of the screen width for focused editing.  
                                                                                
  ### 6. RTF Prompt Engine Integration & Custom Prompt Management               
  - Integrate the template structure of the custom workflow prompts into the    
  core application state (`memoq_harness/prompts.py` and `state.py`).           
  - Expose the Init, MT1, MT2, and MT3 prompt templates in a dedicated "Prompt  
  Management" expander or tab in the UI so the operator can inspect and rewrite 
  the actual system prompt templates dynamically.                               
                                                                                
  ### 7. TXT Rules Export                                                       
  - Remove the "Extra instructions for this run" text area from the Vibe        
  section. Since the main "Vibe / Soft Rules" text box is now fully editable    
  and persists changes directly to `state.json`, a separate temporary input     
  field is redundant.                                                           
  - Implement an explicit "Export Rules to TXT" button in the Left Panel. When  
  clicked, it automatically saves active state into                             
  `C:\memoq-harness\exports\rules_export_[timestamp].txt`.                      
                                                                                
  Please execute these refactoring tasks across the codebase completely, ensure 
  all 130+ tests adapt or pass cleanly, and return a robust production-ready    
  update.

● Found it. These are macOS TextEdit RTFs: paragraph breaks are \ + newline (not
  \par), and \* was being emitted as a literal asterisk so expandedcolortbl
  leaked its semicolons. Both fixable:

      226 +def _map_secondary(                                           
      227 +    primary_list: Sequence[str],                              
      228 +    secondary_list: Sequence[str],                            
      229 +    lookup: Dict[int, str],                                   
      230 +) -> Dict[int, int]:                                          
      231 +    """Map each secondary tag index onto a placeholder number.
      232 +                                                              
      233 +    Exact XML matches bind first, so unchanged tags keep their
          + obvious number.                                              
      234 +    Whatever is left binds positionally, which is the right gu
          +ess when the MT                                               
      235 +    layer has rewritten a tag's attributes but kept the tag or
          +der. Anything                                                 
      236 +    still unmatched gets a number of its own.                 
      237 +    """                                                       
      238 +    free = list(range(len(primary_list)))                     
      239 +    mapping: Dict[int, int] = {}                              
      240 +                                                              
      241 +    for j, xml in enumerate(secondary_list):                  
      242 +        for i in free:                                        
      243 +            if primary_list[i] == xml:                        
      244 +                mapping[j] = i + 1                            
      245 +                free.remove(i)                                
      246 +                break                                         
      247 +                                                              
      248 +    leftovers = [j for j in range(len(secondary_list)) if j no
          +t in mapping]                                                 
      249 +    for j in leftovers:                                       
      250 +        if free:                                              
      251 +            # Prefer the slot at the same ordinal when it is s
          +till available.                                               
      252 +            i = j if j in free else free[0]                   
      253 +            mapping[j] = i + 1                                
      254 +            free.remove(i)                                    
      255 +        else:                                                 
      256 +            number = (max(lookup) + 1) if lookup else 1       
      257 +            lookup[number] = secondary_list[j]                
      258 +            mapping[j] = number                               
      259 +    return mapping                                            
      260 +

      262 +def _render(pieces: Sequence[Tuple[bool, str]], numbers: Seque
          +nce[int], template: str) -> str:                              
      263 +    """Rebuild placeholder text, substituting the assigned tag
          + numbers."""                                                  
      264 +    out: List[str] = []                                       
      265 +    index = 0                                                 
      266 +    for is_tag, payload in pieces:                            
      267 +        if is_tag:                                            
      268 +            out.append(template % numbers[index])             
      269 +            index += 1                                        
      270 +        else:                                                 
      271 +            out.append(unescape_text(payload))                
      272 +    return "".join(out)

      331          force_json: bool,

          +max_tokens)

      337 +                                                              
      338 +    def _chat_anthropic(                                      
      339 +        self,                                                 
      340 +        messages: List[Dict[str, str]],                       
      341 +        force_json: bool,                                     
      342 +        max_tokens: Optional[int] = None,                     
      343 +    ) -> str:                                                 
      344 +        """Anthropic Messages API.                            
      345 +                                                              
      346 +        Three structural differences from the OpenAI shape are
          + handled here:                                                
      347 +        the system prompt is a top-level field rather than a m
          +essage,                                                       
      348 +        ``max_tokens`` is mandatory, and there is no ``respons
          +e_format``. JSON                                              
      349 +        is obtained instead by prefilling the assistant turn w
          +ith ``{``, which                                              
      350 +        constrains the model far more reliably than asking pol
          +itely - the                                                   
      351 +        prefill is stitched back onto the reply before parsing
          +.                                                             
      352 +        """                                                   
      353 +        system = "\n\n".join(m["content"] for m in messages if
          + m["role"] == "system")                                       
      354 +        turns = [                                             
      355 +            {"role": m["role"], "content": m["content"]}      
      356 +            for m in messages                                 
      357 +            if m["role"] != "system"                          
      358 +        ]                                                     
      359 +        if not turns:                                         
      360 +            turns = [{"role": "user", "content": system or "He
          +llo"}]                                                        
      361 +                                                              
      362 +        prefill = force_json                                  
      363 +        if prefill:                                           
      364 +            turns = turns + [{"role": "assistant", "content": 
          +"{"}]                                                         
      365 +                                                              
      366 +        # Anthropic requires a positive max_tokens; there is n
          +o "server decides".                                           
      367 +        limit = self.config.max_tokens if max_tokens is None e
          +lse max_tokens                                                
      368 +        limit = limit if limit and limit > 0 else 4096        
      369 +                                                              
      370          last_error: Optional[Exception] = None
      371 +        for attempt in range(max(1, self.config.max_retries)):
      372 +            try:                                              
      373 +                response = self._client.messages.create(      
      374 +                    model=self.config.model,                  
      375 +                    system=system or anthropic.NOT_GIVEN,     
      376 +                    messages=turns,                           
      377 +                    max_tokens=limit,                         
      378 +                    temperature=self.config.temperature,      
      379 +                )                                             
      380 +            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - SDK rai
          +ses many types                                                
      381 +                last_error = exc                              
      382 +                if attempt + 1 < self.config.max_retries and _
          +is_retryable(str(exc)):                                       
      383 +                    time.sleep(min(2 ** attempt, 8))          
      384 +                    continue                                  
      385 +                raise LLMError(_clean_error(exc)) from exc    
      386 +                                                              
      387 +            text = "".join(                                   
      388 +                block.text for block in getattr(response, "con
          +tent", [])                                                    
      389 +                if getattr(block, "type", "") == "text"       
      390 +            )                                                 
      391 +            if prefill:                                       
      392 +                # Give back what the model would have written 
          +unprompted.                                                   
      393 +                text = "{" + text                             
      394 +            if text.strip():                                  
      395 +                return text                                   
      396 +            last_error = LLMError("Model returned an empty res
          +ponse.")                                                      
      397 +                                                              
      398 +        raise LLMError(_clean_error(last_error) if last_error 
          +else "Request failed.")                                       
      399 +                                                              
      400 +    def _chat_openai(                                         
      401 +        self,                                                 
      402 +        messages: List[Dict[str, str]],                       
      403 +        force_json: bool,                                     
      404 +        max_tokens: Optional[int] = None,                     
      405 +    ) -> str:

      407          want_json = force_json and self._supports_json_mode is

      408  
      409          for attempt in range(max(1, self.config.max_retries)):

      40 +#: Tokens the operator may place in a stage template. Anything 
         +the harness does                                               
      41 +#: not recognise is left exactly as typed, so their own bracket
         +ed notes such as                                               
      42 +#: ``[НОМЕР СТАТЬИ]`` survive untouched for them to fill in by

      43 +TEMPLATE_PLACEHOLDERS = (                                      
      44 +    "{{HARD_RULES}}",                                          
      45 +    "{{VIBE}}",                                                
      46 +    "{{TERMBASE}}",                                            
      47 +    "{{CORRECTIONS}}",                                         
      48 +    "{{SOURCE_LANG}}",

      56 +def stage_for_batch(batch_index: int, batch_count: int) -> str:
      57 +    """Which prompt stage a batch belongs to.                  
      58 +                                                               
      59 +    The templates encode a conversation: ``Init`` sets the mode
         +l up, ``MT1``                                                  
      60 +    opens the article, ``MT2`` is the workhorse that carries co
         +rrections back,                                                
      61 +    ``MT3`` closes the run. Batch 0 is the opening exchange, th
         +e last batch is                                                
      62 +    the closing one, and everything between is MT2.            
      63 +    """                                                        
      64 +    if batch_index <= 0:                                       
      65 +        return "mt1"                                           
      66 +    if batch_count > 1 and batch_index >= batch_count - 1:     
      67 +        return "mt3"                                           
      68 +    return "mt2"                                               
      69 +                                                               
      70 +                                                               
      71 +def render_template(template: str, values: Dict[str, str]) -> T
         +uple[str, Set[str]]:                                           
      72 +    """Substitute known ``{{TOKEN}}`` placeholders in a stage t
         +emplate.                                                       
      73 +                                                               
      74 +    Returns the rendered text plus the set of tokens that were 
         +actually                                                       
      75 +    present, so the caller can avoid emitting the same material
         + twice.                                                        
      76 +    """                                                        
      77 +    used: Set[str] = set()                                     
      78 +    out = template                                             
      79 +    for token in TEMPLATE_PLACEHOLDERS:                        
      80 +        if token in out:                                       
      81 +            used.add(token)                                    
      82 +            out = out.replace(token, values.get(token, "").stri
         +p())                                                           
      83 +    return out, used                                           
      84 +

      952 +                                                              
      953 +def _render_diff(record: state_mod.SegmentRecord) -> None:    
      954 +    """Inline MT-vs-suggestion diff, shown before the operator
          + starts editing."""                                           
      955 +    if not st.session_state.get("show_diff", True):           
      956 +        return                                                
      957 +    if not record.llm_suggestion.strip():                     
      958 +        return                                                
      959 +                                                              
      960 +    change = diffing.stats(record.mt_target, record.llm_sugges
          +tion)                                                         
      961 +    if not change.changed:                                    
      962 +        st.caption(":grey[Model returned the MT unchanged.]") 
      963 +        return                                                
      964 +                                                              
      965 +    st.markdown(                                              
      966 +        diffing.render_html(record.mt_target, record.llm_sugge
          +stion),                                                       
      967 +        unsafe_allow_html=True,                               
      968 +    )                                                         
      969 +    st.caption("MT to suggestion: %s" % change.summary())     
      970 +                                                              
      971 +                                                              
      972 +def _raw_tag_table(segment: mqxliff.Segment) -> str:          
      973 +    """Markdown listing of the real XML behind each placeholde
          +r."""                                                         
      974 +    origin = ("memoQ target (localised)"                      
      975 +              if segment.tag_source == mqxliff.tagmod.TAG_SOUR
          +CE_TARGET                                                     
      976 +              else "source")                                  
      977 +    lines = ["**Raw inline tags** - taken from the %s\n" % ori
          +gin]                                                          
      978 +    for number, xml in segment.raw_tags():                    
      979 +        lines.append("`{%d}`\n```xml\n%s\n```" % (number, xml)
          +)                                                             
      980 +    if not segment.lookup.tags:                               
      981 +        lines.append("_This segment has no inline tags._")    
      982 +    return "\n".join(lines)                                   
      983 +                                                              
      984 +

      986      """Escape markdown so segment text renders literally, tags
            included."""
      987      if not text:

      109 +class TestTagRetargeting(unittest.TestCase):                  
      110 +    """The target owns the tags, so memoQ's localised attribut
          +es survive."""                                                
      111 +                                                              
      112 +    SRC = ('Click <ph id="1">&lt;a href="/en/articles/123"&gt;
          +</ph>here'                                                    
      113 +           '<ph id="2">&lt;/a&gt;</ph>.')                     
      114 +    TGT = ('Нажмите <ph id="1">&lt;a href="/ru/articles/123"&g
          +t;</ph>здесь'

      116 +                                                              
      117 +    def test_target_supplies_the_canonical_xml(self):         
      118 +        source, target, lookup = tags.isolate_pair(self.SRC, s
          +elf.TGT)                                                      
      119 +        self.assertIn("/ru/articles/123", lookup.tags[1])     
      120 +        self.assertNotIn("/en/", lookup.tags[1])              
      121 +        # Both sides still show the same placeholder numbers. 
      122 +        self.assertEqual(source.placeholder_text, "Click {1}he
          +re{2}.")                                                      
      123 +        self.assertEqual(target.placeholder_text, "Нажмите {1}
          +здесь{2}.")                                                   
      124 +                                                              
      125 +    def test_localised_link_survives_a_round_trip(self):      
      126 +        _, target, lookup = tags.isolate_pair(self.SRC, self.T
          +GT)                                                           
      127 +        restored, report = tags.restore(                      
      128 +            target.placeholder_text, lookup, expected=sorted(t
          +arget.tags))                                                  
      129 +        self.assertTrue(report.ok)                            
      130 +        self.assertIn("/ru/articles/123", restored)           
      131 +        self.assertNotIn("/en/articles/123", restored)        
      132 +                                                              
      133 +    def test_source_canonical_mode_reverts_the_link(self):    
      134 +        """Documents the old behaviour this change exists to f
          +ix."""                                                        
      135 +        source, target, lookup = tags.isolate_pair(

      138 +            target.placeholder_text, lookup, expected=sorted(s
          +ource.tags))

      140 +                                                              
      141 +    def test_empty_target_falls_back_to_source(self):

          +")

           + энтропия")

       936 +    def test_placeholders_are_single_tokens(self):           
       937 +        runs = diffing.word_diff("{1}текст", "{1}текст")     
       938 +        self.assertEqual(runs, [(diffing.EQUAL, "{1}текст")])
       939 +        self.assertIn("{1}", diffing.tokenize("a {1} b"))    
       940 +                                                             
       941 +    def test_moved_placeholder_is_detected(self):            
       942 +        change = diffing.stats("{1}Save{2} now", "now {1}Save
           +{2}")                                                        
       943 +        self.assertTrue(change.changed)

           +т")                                                          
       947 +        self.assertIn("line-through", out)      # deletion   
       948 +        self.assertIn("border-bottom", out)     # insertion  
       949 +        self.assertIn("текст", out)                          
       950 +                                                             
       951 +    def test_html_escapes_markup(self):                      
       952 +        out = diffing.render_html("a", "<script>x</script>") 
       953 +        self.assertNotIn("<script>", out)                    
       954 +        self.assertIn("&lt;script&gt;", out)                 
       955 +                                                             
       956 +    def test_html_is_length_capped(self):                    
       957 +        out = diffing.render_html("a " * 5000, "b " * 5000, m
           +ax_chars=200)                                                
       958 +        self.assertLess(len(out), 3000)                      
       959 +                                                             
       960 +    def test_markdown_fallback(self):                        
       961 +        out = diffing.render_markdown("старый", "новый")     
       962 +        self.assertIn("~~старый~~", out)                     
       963 +        self.assertIn("**новый**", out)

       998 +    def test_system_prompt_is_hoisted_out_of_messages(self): 
       999 +        """Anthropic takes `system` as a top-level field, not
           + a message."""                                               
      1000 +        captured = {}                                        
      1001 +                                                             
      1002 +        class FakeMessages:                                  
      1003 +            def create(self, **kwargs):                      
      1004 +                captured.update(kwargs)                      
      1005 +                return type("R", (), {"content": [           
      1006 +                    type("B", (), {"type": "text", "text": '"
           +a": 1}'})()]})()                                             
      1007 +                                                             
      1008 +        client = llm.LLMClient.__new__(llm.LLMClient)        
      1009 +        client.config = llm.ClientConfig(                    
      1010 +            backend=llm.BACKEND_ANTHROPIC, model="claude-3-5-
           +sonnet-latest")                                              
      1011 +        client._supports_json_mode = None                    
      1012 +        client._client = type("C", (), {"messages": FakeMessa
           +ges()})()                                                    
      1013 +                                                             
      1014 +        out = client._chat(                                  
      1015 +            [{"role": "system", "content": "SYS"},           
      1016 +             {"role": "user", "content": "USER"}],           
      1017 +            force_json=True,                                 
      1018 +        )                                                    
      1019 +        self.assertEqual(captured["system"], "SYS")          
      1020 +        self.assertEqual(captured["messages"][0], {"role": "u
           +ser", "content": "USER"})                                    
      1021 +        # JSON is coaxed with an assistant prefill, which is 
           +stitched back on.                                            
      1022 +        self.assertEqual(captured["messages"][-1], {"role": "
           +assistant", "content": "{"})                                 
      1023 +        self.assertEqual(out, '{"a": 1}')                    
      1024 +        self.assertGreater(captured["max_tokens"], 0)        
      1025 +                                                             
      1026 +    def test_max_tokens_is_always_positive(self):            
      1027 +        """Anthropic rejects the request without it; there is
           + no server default."""                                       
      1028 +        captured = {}

           +ce_json=False)

      68 +#: segments would otherwise stall the whole Streamlit run - a f
         +rozen UI is a                                                  
      69 +#: far worse outcome than a coarse diff. Real memoQ segments ar
         +e a sentence or                                                
      70 +#: two, so this ceiling is never reached in normal use.        
      71 +MAX_DIFF_TOKENS = 2000                                         
      72 +                                                               
      73 +                                                               
      74  def tokenize(text: str) -> List[str]:
      75      return _TOKEN_RE.findall(text or "")
      76  
     ...
      82      span per change rather than one per word.
      83      """
      84      old_tokens, new_tokens = tokenize(old), tokenize(new)
      85 +                                                               
      86 +    if len(old_tokens) + len(new_tokens) > MAX_DIFF_TOKENS:    
      87 +        # Coarse fallback: report the pair as wholly replaced. 
      88 +        coarse: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []                     
      89 +        if old:                                                
      90 +            coarse.append((DELETE, old))                       
      91 +        if new:                                                
      92 +            coarse.append((INSERT, new))                       
      93 +        return coarse                                          
      94 +                                                               
      95      matcher = difflib.SequenceMatcher(a=old_tokens, b=new_token
          s, autojunk=False)
      96  
      97      runs: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []

       96  export disagrees. The sidebar always reports how many rows wer
